# The long game: knight progression

What a solo knight builds besides levels, 50 levels at a time: the quests that open hunting grounds or add value, the Bestiary (charm points), the Bosstiary (boss points), and what buyers see on knights sold at each level. Start with the checklist: tick what your character has done, then plan the next targets. It's also the review to do when you buy a character. Premium account and solo play assumed.

**In short:**
- There are 833 creatures: 1,247,165 kills and 28,734 charm points to finish them all. 69% of those kills are Hard and Challenging creatures, which need a high level. Finishing everything is a goal of years, so go for the most charm points per hour in your level band.
- Below level 100 there are 374 creatures to finish for 7,102 charm points. Doing the fastest ones first, your first 300 hours of Bestiary hunting there give about 1,489 charm points; a few slow ones take hundreds of hours each, so leave those for later.
- **Beastly** (2,000 charm points) takes about 603 hours with creatures below level 50, fastest first.
- Join the **Paw and Fur** hunters in Port Hope from level 6 and get an **Adventurer's Stone**: the tasks pay experience and open bosses while the same kills fill your Bestiary.
- Hunt in a party when you can: everyone who hit a creature in the 5 minutes before it died gets the kill.

## Checklist
Print it, or copy it into a file, and tick what your character has done. For a character you just bought, go down it with its Bazaar page and, in game, the quest log and the Cyclopedia (Bestiary, Bosstiary, charms) open. The level is when it fits a solo knight; the bands below give the details. Start with the **Essential** list; the **Comprehensive** one adds the rest worth doing on a character you keep.

### Essential
The core, up to level 80. Each item opens a hunting ground the main guides use, is a system their numbers assume (promotion, imbuements, the Wheel), or shows on the Bazaar, where sold knights that had it fetched more. Most take under 2 hours. It's also the list to finish before you sell a knight.

Small rewards, few Bestiary creatures, no hunting ground the guides recommend above level 60, and no clear price difference on the Bazaar. The Tomes of Knowledge some of them give are also on the Market.

## How the Bestiary works
Each finished entry gives **charm points**, which unlock charms (extra damage, healing, dodging and more against the creatures you put them on), and finishing whole classes gives titles.

Kill a creature once to open its entry. More kills unlock its details in three stages, and the last stage gives the charm points. Very rare creatures (the ones that show up only now and then) need just 5 kills and give more points.

| Difficulty | First stage | Second stage | Final stage | Charm points | Very rare: points | Creatures | Kills for all | Points for all |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| Harmless | 5 | 10 | 25 | 1 | 5 | 20 (1 very rare) | 480 | 24 |
| Trivial | 10 | 100 | 250 | 5 | 10 | 50 (4 very rare) | 11,520 | 270 |
| Easy | 25 | 250 | 500 | 15 | 30 | 173 (8 very rare) | 82,540 | 2,715 |
| Medium | 50 | 500 | 1,000 | 25 | 50 | 310 (25 very rare) | 285,125 | 8,375 |
| Hard | 100 | 1,000 | 2,500 | 50 | 100 | 213 (0 very rare) | 532,500 | 10,650 |
| Challenging | 200 | 2,000 | 5,000 | 100 | 200 | 67 (0 very rare) | 335,000 | 6,700 |
| **All** | | | | | | **833** | **1,247,165** | **28,734** |

**What counts as a kill:** you must have hit the creature in the last 5 minutes before it died. You don't need to deal the most damage, so everyone in a party who hits it gets the kill.

**Ways to go faster:**
- **Bounty Tasks** (Task Board): the Bounty Talisman can be upgraded to give up to 100% chance of double Bestiary progress against your bounty creature, along with more damage, life leech and loot against it. Pick bounty creatures you still need.
- **Weekly kill tasks** (Task Board): up to 6 a week. They pay experience and hunting task points, and the kills count for the Bestiary too.
- **Boosted creature:** every server save one creature gets double experience, double loot and twice the respawn, so more of it to kill. If it's one you need, that's the day to hunt it.
- **Paw and Fur tasks** (below): the same kills count for the task and for the Bestiary.
- **Echo Raids:** now and then a creature leaves an echo behind; setting it off brings 8 more of that creature (or 4 influenced ones) for 10 minutes, all counting for the Bestiary. About 1 time in 10, or 1 in 5 once that entry is finished, an **Echo Warden** comes instead, and the first one you help kill for each creature gives extra charm points: 1 Harmless, 2 Trivial, 5 Easy, 10 Medium, 15 Hard, 30 Challenging.
- **Charms on what you hunt next:** a finished entry lets you put a charm on that creature. Finishing the easy creatures first gives you charm points for the hard ones.

### Titles
| Title | How |
|---|---|
| Beastly | 2,000 charm points |
| One title per class (see "Class titles" below) | every entry of that class |
| Executioner | every Bestiary entry |
| Master / Mistress of Souls | the Animus Mastery of every Bestiary creature (Soulpit) |
| Prince / Princess Charming | the most charm points on your world |

## Guilds and societies
### Adventurers' Guild
Use an **Adventurer's Stone** inside any city's temple to go to the Adventurers' Guild, and from there to any big city. You get the first stone on the Island of Destiny ship, or ask a temple NPC for an "Adventurers' Stone". It's the cheapest way to hop between the cities this guide sends you to.

### Paw and Fur – Hunting Elite (Port Hope)
Talk to **Grizzly Adams** in Port Hope and ask for a **task**. He offers kill tasks by level; each finished task gives experience and points, points give ranks, and most tasks open a boss you can then fight. Up to 3 of his tasks can run at once. For a task, a kill counts when you dealt the most damage (in a shared-experience party, everyone who hit it). Players of level 130+ get a few bonus points while they have under 70.

| Rank | Points | Level | What it gives |
|---|---|---|---|
| Huntsman | 10 | 6 | 5,000 exp; sell Cyclops, Minotaur, Lizard and Draken trophies and some creature products to Grizzly Adams |
| Ranger | 20 | 6 | 8,000 exp; Lorek's boat from Port Hope to Banuta, Chor and the mountain pass to Darama |
| Big Game Hunter | 40 | 50 | 10,000 exp; sell Orc, Deer, Lion, Wolf and Bonebeast trophies |
| Trophy Hunter | 70 | 80 | 15,000 exp; Demon Backpack; sell more trophies; special tasks (Tiquandas Revenge from 90, Demodras from 100) |
| Elite Hunter | 100 | 130 | 30,000 exp; the 6,666 Demons task |

### Tibian Explorer Society
A society of explorers with outposts in Port Hope, Northport, Liberty Bay, Svargrond and Yalahar; join in Port Hope or Northport. Its quest line ([The Explorer Society Quest](https://tibia.fandom.com/wiki/The_Explorer_Society_Quest)) sends you around the world and opens some places along the way (TibiaWiki links it to Calassa and Chor).

## The path, 50 levels at a time
Each band lists the creatures whose level falls in it, best charm points per hour first. **Level** is where hunting it makes sense (see the appendix for how it's set); **Kills/h** counts only that creature at that hunting ground, and **Hours** is what finishing it takes there. The other creatures you kill on the way count for their own entries. "?" means the model has no estimate.

### Levels 0–50
254 creatures, 143,875 kills, 3,910 charm points, about 2,964 hours (26 without an estimate). Running total: 3,910 charm points, 2,964 hours.

**Quests**

With friends (team quests): [The Desert Dungeon](https://tibia.fandom.com/wiki/The_Desert_Dungeon_Quest) (5% of the sold knights here).

**Bane bosses within reach**, placed by HP (about 20 HP a level; check each one's damage on TibiaWiki before you go): Jailer (65 HP), Diseased Dan (800 HP), Heoni (900 HP), Herald of Fire (900 HP).

**What buyers see**, from 194 knights sold at these levels (median, and the top quarter): charm points 0 (top quarter 15); boss points 0 (top quarter 0); quest lines 1 (top quarter 1); achievement points 2 (top quarter 4); mounts 0 (top quarter 0); outfits 12 (top quarter 12).

### Levels 50–100
120 creatures, 134,550 kills, 3,192 charm points, about 2,645 hours (16 without an estimate). Running total: 7,102 charm points, 5,609 hours.

**What buyers see**, from 682 knights sold at these levels (median, and the top quarter): charm points 80 (top quarter 145); boss points 0 (top quarter 0); quest lines 2 (top quarter 2); achievement points 13 (top quarter 21); mounts 1 (top quarter 2); outfits 12 (top quarter 13).

### Levels 100–150
86 creatures, 105,000 kills, 2,460 charm points, about 2,502 hours (9 without an estimate). Running total: 9,562 charm points, 8,111 hours.

**Quests**

| Quest | Level | Effort | Opens | Gives | Sold knights here with it |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| [The Hunt for the Sea Serpent](https://tibia.fandom.com/wiki/The_Hunt_for_the_Sea_Serpent_Quest) | 61 / 100 |  | Sea Serpent Area (Captain Haba's boat); comfortable without potions from about level 500 (the 100+ guide) |  | – |
| [Shadows of Yalahar](https://tibia.fandom.com/wiki/Shadows_of_Yalahar_Quest) | 75 / 110 | ~6 h | trade with Telas; a Golem Wrench, to tame the Gnarlhound mount; War Golems in missions 6 and 9; Black Bert sells the Old Power Core and the Golem Head, which skip the fights of missions 7 and 12 | golem items | 3% |
| [The New Frontier](https://tibia.fandom.com/wiki/The_New_Frontier_Quest) | 77 / 110+ | ~4–6 h, an arena fight; start it early: it opens Farmine's elevator doors | Farmine and Zao (Muggy Plains, Chaochai, Zzaion…) | Warmaster outfit | 15% |
| [Threatened Dreams](https://tibia.fandom.com/wiki/Threatened_Dreams_Quest) | 30 / 100 | ~4–6 h, some steps at in-game night | Feyrist (Feyrist Meadows) | Butterfly Ring | 0% |
| [Twenty Miles Beneath the Sea](https://tibia.fandom.com/wiki/Twenty_Miles_Beneath_the_Sea_Quest) | 0 / 130 | ~2 h | Seacrest Grounds | Mind the Step! and Snake Charmer achievements | 0% |
| [The Ice Islands](https://tibia.fandom.com/wiki/The_Ice_Islands_Quest) | 0 / 130+ | long, with waits between missions | Nibelor (mission 1), Tyrsung (Nibelor 2), Formorgar Mines (mission 9), Helheim | Norseman outfit | 3% |
| [The Explorer Society](https://tibia.fandom.com/wiki/The_Explorer_Society_Quest) | 0 / 110+ | ~8 h+, many missions | teleports Liberty Bay ↔ Svargrond and Port Hope ↔ Northport; Captain Max's boat to Calassa; the Spectral Dress mission needs a talk with The Queen of the Banshees |  | 3% |
| [In Service of Yalahar: missions 9–10 (Azerus)](https://tibia.fandom.com/wiki/In_Service_of_Yalahar_Quest) | 80 / 120 | pick a side, then kill Azerus within about 12 minutes; a team helps near level 80 |  | a Yalahari armor piece, Yalaharian outfit | 6% |
| [Bigfoot's Burden](https://tibia.fandom.com/wiki/Bigfoot's_Burden_Quest) | 80 / 120 | days of gnome rank tasks | Gnomegate, the Spike, Crystal Grounds; Mushroom Gardens at rank II |  | – |
| [Grimvale](https://tibia.fandom.com/wiki/Grimvale_Quest) | 0 / 100 | only around the full moon (12th–14th) | Last Sanctum and the Foxtail Amulet (The Curse Spreads: its 5 were-beast leaders are fought alone), Lion Sanctum shortcuts | Foxtail Amulet | – |
| [The Pits of Inferno: the first missions](https://tibia.fandom.com/wiki/The_Pits_of_Inferno_Quest) | 80 / 130+ | Key 3700 (Bonelords and Ghouls south-west of Venore), the Holy Tible, then blood on Goshnar's grave | Pits of Inferno Dragon Lair (Dragon Lords) |  | – |
| [The Ape City](https://tibia.fandom.com/wiki/The_Ape_City_Quest) | 0 / 130+ | ~4–6 h | Deeper Banuta, the Forbidden Lands | Shaman outfit | 9% |
| [Liquid Black](https://tibia.fandom.com/wiki/Liquid_Black_Quest) | 0 / 130+ | ~3–5 h | deeper Fiehonja and the Deeplings Library | Deepling outfit | – |

**Bane bosses within reach**, placed by HP (about 20 HP a level; check each one's damage on TibiaWiki before you go): Mad Mage (2,500 HP).

**What buyers see**, from 360 knights sold at these levels (median, and the top quarter): charm points 185 (top quarter 320); boss points 0 (top quarter 10); quest lines 3 (top quarter 5); achievement points 28 (top quarter 49); mounts 2 (top quarter 3); outfits 13 (top quarter 14).

### Levels 400–450

## Bosstiary
The Bosstiary is the Bestiary for bosses. Each boss has three stages; each stage gives boss points, and boss points raise the extra equipment loot of the boss you put in your boss slot (a second slot comes at 1,500 points). There are 316 bosses for 27,450 boss points.

| Category | What it means | Kills for the 3 stages | Boss points | Bosses |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| Bane | can be killed more than once a day | 25 / 100 / 300 | 5 + 15 + 30 = 50 | 83 |
| Archfoe | once every 20 to 48 hours | 5 / 20 / 60 | 10 + 30 + 60 = 100 | 124 |
| Nemesis | rarer, or a longer cooldown | 1 / 3 / 5 | 10 + 30 + 60 = 100 | 109 |

- **Bane bosses are the farm:** they can be repeated, so their 25 / 100 / 300 kills are doable, and many sit at the end of a quest you do anyway. Prowess (the first stage) on any boss also opens your first boss slot.
- Every server save one Archfoe is the **boosted boss**: killing it that day counts 3 kills.
- The Paw and Fur task bosses (The Snapper, Hide, The Many…) are not in the Bosstiary on TibiaWiki: they give achievements and loot, not boss points.

## Appendix: how the numbers are made
1. **The creatures:** every TibiaWiki Bestiary entry (difficulty, class, HP, location) and the Bestiary table of kills and points.
2. **Where:** TibiaWiki's hunting ground pages (which creatures live there, the suggested knight level, how to get in), placed on the Canary open-source game map to find each ground's spawns. Creatures no ground page reaches get a ground of their own around their densest spawns.
3. **Level:** TibiaWiki's suggested knight level for the hunting ground where you'd hunt the creature (among those where it makes at least a fifth of the kills, the lowest-level one giving at least 70% of its best kill rate), but never under 1 level per 40 of its HP: bigger monsters take too long to kill before that.
4. **Kills per hour:** the same model as the knight hunting guide (fight by fight: your hits after their armor, Shield Bash on your target, `exori` and `exori gran` on boxes as far as your mana lasts with no potions, walking, looting, respawn), with the guide's typical knight; from level 200 also a second Basic Void (on the weapon) and the Wheel's extra HP. Past level 200 the gear stays at the level-200 picks and the skill grows slowly (about 105 at level 500), so high levels are on the slow side. Hours = kills needed ÷ that creature's kills per hour.
5. **Quests:** the knight hunting guide's checked access list, and TibiaWiki's notes for the other hunting grounds.

**How far to trust them:**
- Kills per hour come from the map's spawns and the model, checked against real hunts only for the main guide's hunting grounds.
- 189 creatures have no estimate: the newest areas aren't in the open map data, and some monsters are out of the model's reach (they heal faster than the typical knight hits, or physical damage barely hurts them).
- Other players in the same hunting ground, daily boosts and rapid-respawn events are not counted.
- Shield Bash damage is an estimate (no published formula yet).

Sources: TibiaWiki (Bestiary, Bosstiary, hunting grounds, [Killing in the Name of... Quest](https://tibia.fandom.com/wiki/Killing_in_the_Name_of..._Quest), Task Board, creatures, quests), the Canary open-source server (map spawns, creature attacks), and the knight hunting guide's model.
